The discharge associated with the measurement made on Dec. 19, 2019, was revised on Aug. 4, 2025, based on further review of the measurement. Measurements made on Feb. 26, 2020, July 1, 2020, and Sept. 19, 2023, were deemed in error on Aug. 4, 2025, and removed from publication, based on further review of the measurements. Select gage height values for the event of Nov. 1, 2019, were deemed in error and removed from publication on Aug. 4, 2025, based on a poorly functioning stilling well. Discharge for the period Nov. 1, 2019, to Apr. 3, 2025, was revised on Aug. 4, 2025, based on changes to the stage-discharge rating; this revision supersedes the previous revision to this period. The discharge associated with the measurement made on Mar. 31, 1951, was revised on Apr. 22, 2026, based on further review of the measurement.

EAST CANADA CREEK AT EAST CREEK NY, USGS site 01348000 (USGS-01348000), stream in Herkimer County, New York, draining 289 square miles. Daily means from 1945-12-02 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
